Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n93\t\n94\t**用途**：显示模型 confidence bin vs accuracy，对角线 = 完美校准。\n95\t\n96\t```python\n97\tdef plot_reliability_diagram(confidences, accuracies, n_bins=10,\n98\t                              label='', color='#0072B2',\n99\t                              title='Reliability Diagram',\n100\t                              outpath='reliability.pdf'):\n101\t    \"\"\"\n102\t    confidences, accuracies: 各样本的 confidence 和 binary correct (0/1)\n103\t    \"\"\"\n104\t    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n105\t    bin_accs, bin_confs, bin_counts = [], [], []\n106\t\n107\t    for i in range(n_bins):\n108\t        lo, hi = bin_edges[i], bin_edges[i+1]\n109\t        mask = (confidences >= lo) & (confidences < hi)\n110\t        if mask.sum() > 0:\n111\t            bin_accs.append(accuracies[mask].mean())\n112\t            bin_confs.append(confidences[mask].mean())\n113\t            bin_counts.append(mask.sum())\n114\t        else:\n115\t            bin_accs.append(float('nan'))\n116\t            bin_confs.append((lo + hi) / 2)\n117\t            bin_counts.append(0)\n118\t\n119\t    bin_accs = np.array(bin_accs)\n120\t    bin_confs = np.array(bin_confs)\n121\t\n122\t    fig, (ax_main, ax_hist) = plt.subplots(\n123\t        2, 1, figsize=(4, 4.5), gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [3, 1]},\n124\t        sharex=True\n125\t    )\n126\t\n127\t    # 主图：gaps\n128\t    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n129\t                color=color, alpha=0.7, label='Accuracy', zorder=2)\n130\t    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_confs - bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n131\t                bottom=bin_accs, color='red', alpha=0.3, label='Gap', zorder=2)\n132\t    ax_main.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', linewidth=1, zorder=3, label='Perfect')\n133\t    ax_main.set_xlim(0, 1); ax_main.set_ylim(0, 1)\n134\t    ax_main.set_ylabel('Accuracy')\n135\t    ax_main.set_title(title)\n136\t    ax_main.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9)\n137\t    ax_main.grid(True, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n138\t\n139\t    # ECE annotation\n140\t    ece = np.nansum(np.array(bin_counts) / sum(bin_counts) *\n141\t                    np.abs(bin_accs - bin_confs))\n142\t    ax_main.text(0.98, 0.02, f'ECE={ece:.3f}', transform=ax_main.transAxes,\n143\t                 ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n144\t                 bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='white', alpha=0.8))\n145\t\n146\t    # 直方图（样本密度）\n147\t    ax_hist.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_counts, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n148\t                color=color, alpha=0.5)\n149\t    ax_hist.set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=9)\n150\t    ax_hist.set_xlabel('Confidence')\n151\t\n152\t    plt.tight_layout()\n153\t    plt.savefig(outpath)\n154\t    plt.close()\n155\t    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n156\t```\n157\t\n158\t### 批量生成 6 张对比图（EMNLP 论文用）\n159\t\n160\t选图策略：每 Regime 选最大 ΔECE 的量化方法 vs FP16，LLaMA + Qwen3 各一。\n161\t\n162\t```python\n163\t# 在 A6000 服务器上执行\n164\tFIGURE_PLAN = [\n165\t    # (dataset, model, quant, regime, title)\n166\t    ('hellaswag',   'llama',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — LLaMA'),\n167\t    ('hellaswag',   'qwen3',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — Qwen3'),\n168\t    ('arc_c',       'llama',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — LLaMA'),\n169\t    ('arc_c',       'qwen3',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — Qwen3'),\n170\t    ('truthfulqa',  'llama',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — LLaMA'),\n171\t    ('truthfulqa',  'qwen3',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — Qwen3'),\n172\t]\n173\t```\n174\t\n175\t---\n176\t\n177\t## 2. ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n178\t\n179\t**用途**：Main Results Table 的可视化版本，颜色 = ΔECE（红=恶化，蓝=改善）。\n180\t\n181\t```python\n182\tdef plot_ece_heatmap(delta_ece_matrix, methods, datasets,\n183\t                     title='ΔECE Heatmap (vs FP16)',\n184\t                     outpath='ece_heatmap.pdf'):\n185\t    \"\"\"\n186\t    delta_ece_matrix: shape (n_methods, n_datasets), 正=ECE 恶化, 负=改善\n187\t    \"\"\"\n188\t    import matplotlib.colors as mcolors\n189\t\n190\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(len(datasets) * 1.2, len(methods) * 0.8 + 1))\n191\t\n192\t    # 发散色图（中心=0, 红=恶化, 蓝=改善）\n193\t    vmax = np.nanmax(np.abs(delta_ece_matrix))\n194\t    norm = mcolors.TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-vmax, vcenter=0, vmax=vmax)\n195\t    cmap = plt.cm.RdBu_r\n196\t\n197\t    im = ax.imshow(delta_ece_matrix, cmap=cmap, norm=norm, aspect='auto')\n198\t\n199\t    # 数值标注\n200\t    for i in range(len(methods)):\n201\t        for j in range(len(datasets)):\n202\t            val = delta_ece_matrix[i, j]\n203\t            if not np.isnan(val):\n204\t                color = 'white' if abs(val) > vmax * 0.6 else 'black'\n205\t                sign = '+' if val > 0 else ''\n206\t                ax.text(j, i, f'{sign}{val:.1%}', ha='center', va='center',\n207\t                        fontsize=9, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n208\t\n209\t    ax.set_xticks(range(len(datasets)))\n210\t    ax.set_yticks(range(len(methods)))\n211\t    ax.set_xticklabels(datasets, rotation=30, ha='right')\n212\t    ax.set_yticklabels(methods)\n213\t    ax.set_title(title)\n214\t\n215\t    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)\n216\t    cbar.set_label('ΔECE (quantized − FP16)')\n217\t    cbar.ax.axhline(0, color='k', linewidth=0.8)\n218\t\n219\t    plt.tight_layout()\n220\t    plt.savefig(outpath)\n221\t    plt.close()\n222\t    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n223\t```\n224\t\n225\t---\n226\t\n227\t## 3. ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n366\t                markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n367\t                markeredgewidth=1.2, zorder=4)\n368\t        ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), textcoords='offset points',\n369\t                    xytext=(0, 7), fontsize=5, color=color,\n370\t                    ha='center', fontstyle='italic')\n371\t```\n372\t\n373\t### Pattern E：Wilson Binomial CI\n374\t\n375\t```python\n376\tdef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n377\t    if n == 0: return 0, 0\n378\t    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n379\t    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n380\t    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n381\t    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n382\t\n383\t# 用法\n384\tlo, hi = wilson_ci(accuracy, n)\n385\tax.errorbar(x, accuracy*100, yerr=[[accuracy*100 - lo*100], [hi*100 - accuracy*100]],\n386\t            fmt='o', capsize=4, capthick=0.8, elinewidth=0.8,\n387\t            markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n388\t```\n389\t\n390\t### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（防标注重叠）\n391\t\n392\t```python\n393\tlabel_offsets = {\n394\t    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),\n395\t    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),\n396\t    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n397\t}\n398\tfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n399\t    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n400\t    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy),\n401\t                textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7,\n402\t                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color='gray',\n403\t                                lw=0.4) if abs(ox) > 20 else None)\n404\t```\n405\t\n406\t### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n407\t\n408\t```python\n409\tfrom matplotlib.patches import Patch\n410\tfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n411\t    if is_collapse:\n412\t        bar.set_hatch('///')\n413\t        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n414\t# Legend 加 hatching 说明\n415\tlegend_patch = Patch(facecolor='gray', hatch='///', alpha=0.7, label='Collapse')\n416\tax.legend(handles=[legend_patch], loc='lower right')\n417\t```\n418\t\n419\t### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n420\t\n421\t```python\n422\tfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n423\t                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n424\t# axes[0] 是主面板（1.4x 宽），axes[1] 副面板\n425\t# 给每个 panel 写 insight subtitle\n426\taxes[0].set_title('(a) Quantization shifts confidence', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n427\taxes[1].set_title('(b) Temperature scaling recovers it', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n428\t```\n429\t\n430\t### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n431\t\n432\t```python\n433\td = 0.012  # 斜线长度\n434\tkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black',\n435\t              clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\n436\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)\n437\tax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)\n438\t```\n439\t\n440\t### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n441\t\n442\t```python\n443\timport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n444\tt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\n445\tt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n446\t```\n447\t\n448\t### Pattern K：Concept curve（合成轨迹建立直觉）\n449\t\n450\t```python\n451\t# 完全不基于实验数据的概念曲线\n452\tx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\n453\ttrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\n454\tax.plot(x, trajectory, color='#0072B2', linewidth=2,\n455\t        solid_capstyle='round', label='Theoretical trajectory')\n456\t\n457\t# 关键节点用 insight 标注\n458\tax.annotate('gap eliminated', xy=(1.0, final_y),\n459\t            xytext=(0.7, final_y - 5), color='#388E3C',\n460\t            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9,\n461\t            arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#388E3C', lw=0.8))\n462\t```\n463\t\n464\t### Pattern L：Forest Plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n465\t\n466\t```python\n467\t# 3-column GridSpec: label | CI plot | inline stats\n468\tfig = plt.figure(figsize=(7, 3.5))\n469\tgs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[0.8, 2, 1.2], wspace=0.1)\n470\tax_label = fig.add_subplot(gs[0]); ax_label.axis('off')\n471\tax_main = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\n472\tax_stat = fig.add_subplot(gs[2]); ax_stat.axis('off')\n473\t\n474\t# 等价带（半透明 axvspan）\n475\tax_main.axvspan(-1, 1, alpha=0.1, color='gray', label='±1pp equivalence')\n476\t\n477\t# 每行：dot + horizontal CI line\n478\tfor i, (name, mean, lo, hi, pval) in enumerate(rows):\n479\t    ax_label.text(0.5, i, name, fontsize=9, va='center', ha='center')\n480\t    ax_main.plot([lo, hi], [i, i], color='black', linewidth=1)\n481\t    ax_main.plot(mean, i, 'D', color='#0072B2', markersize=7)\n482\t    ax_stat.text(0.5, i, f'{mean:+.2f} [{lo:+.2f}, {hi:+.2f}]  p={pval:.3f}',\n483\t                 fontsize=8, va='center', ha='center', family='monospace')\n484\t\n485\tax_main.axvline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.6)\n486\tax_main.invert_yaxis()\n487\t```\n488\t\n489\t---\n490\t\n491\t## 6. 图表 Checklist（提交前）\n492\t\n493\t### L1 防丑（底线）\n494\t- [ ] 300 DPI（`savefig.dpi=300`）+ PDF 矢量\n495\t- [ ] 色盲友好配色（Okabe-Ito 或语义色板）\n496\t- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n497\t- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width matplotlib\nversion: 2\ntools:\n  - ssh_execute\n---\n\n# Draw Paper Figure — 学术统计图生成（Worker 执行）\n\nWorker 收到统计图画图任务时读本 skill。**画图前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的 L1/L2 原则和 L3 pattern 库。\n\n**质量底线：每张图必须达到 NeurIPS/ICML/EMNLP best paper 的视觉水准——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感（灰背景、粗网格线、tab10 色板、默认蓝色）。**\n\n**生成图片后必须 Read PNG 做视觉检查，至少迭代 3 轮（Hero Figure 至少 5 轮）。第一版画出来基本不能直接用。**\n\n本 skill 提供：\n- 通用样式（rcParams + 色板）\n- 三个常用图表的起点模板（reliability/heatmap/scatter）\n- NeurIPS 经验提炼的 pattern 实现示例\n\n## 输入字段（从 Paper Agent 传入）\n\n- `fig_id` / `type` / `placement` / `data_source` / `aspect_ratio` / 输出路径\n- **`narrative_intent`**：一句话，这张图要让读者相信什么\n- **`design_notes`**：从 paper-visualization L3 选的 pattern 列表\n\n## 画图工作流\n\n1. 读 paper-visualization skill 的 L1 防丑原则 + L2 叙事原则\n2. 根据 `narrative_intent` 选起点模板（本 skill §模板）\n3. 按 `design_notes` 中每个 pattern 添加相应技法（本 skill §pattern 实现）\n4. 提交前过本 skill §checklist\n\n## 通用样式标准（所有图表必须遵守）\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nimport matplotlib.patches as mpatches\nimport numpy as np\n\n# 字体和全局样式\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'DejaVu Sans',\n    'font.size': 11,\n    'axes.titlesize': 13,\n    'axes.labelsize': 12,\n    'xtick.labelsize': 10,\n    'ytick.labelsize': 10,\n    'legend.fontsize': 10,\n    'figure.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.dpi': 300,\n    'savefig.bbox': 'tight',\n    'savefig.pad_inches': 0.05,\n    'axes.spines.top': False,\n    'axes.spines.right': False,\n    'lines.linewidth': 1.8,\n})\n\n# 色盲友好配色：优先从 writing_plan.yaml 的 color_palette 读取\n# 读不到则 fallback 到以下默认值\nCOLORS = {\n    'fp16':   '#E69F00',  # 橙黄\n    'nf4':    '#56B4E9',  # 天蓝\n    'int8':   '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'gptq':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n    'awq':    '#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'llama':  '#0072B2',  # 深蓝\n    'qwen3':  '#D55E00',  # 橙红\n    'gemma':  '#009E73',  # 绿\n    'mistral':'#CC79A7',  # 粉紫\n    'phi3':   '#F0E442',  # 黄\n}\n\n# 方法标签\nMETHOD_LABELS = {\n    'fp16': 'FP16', 'nf4': 'BNB-NF4',\n    'int8': 'BNB-INT8', 'gptq': 'GPTQ-4bit', 'awq': 'AWQ-4bit'\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 1. Reliability Diagram（校准图）\n\n**用途**：显示模型 confidence bin vs accuracy，对角线 = 完美校准。\n\n```python\ndef plot_reliability_diagram(confidences, accuracies, n_bins=10,\n                              label='', color='#0072B2',\n                              title='Reliability Diagram',\n                              outpath='reliability.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    confidences, accuracies: 各样本的 confidence 和 binary correct (0/1)\n    \"\"\"\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    bin_accs, bin_confs, bin_counts = [], [], []\n\n    for i in range(n_bins):\n        lo, hi = bin_edges[i], bin_edges[i+1]\n        mask = (confidences >= lo) & (confidences < hi)\n        if mask.sum() > 0:\n            bin_accs.append(accuracies[mask].mean())\n            bin_confs.append(confidences[mask].mean())\n            bin_counts.append(mask.sum())\n        else:\n            bin_accs.append(float('nan'))\n            bin_confs.append((lo + hi) / 2)\n            bin_counts.append(0)\n\n    bin_accs = np.array(bin_accs)\n    bin_confs = np.array(bin_confs)\n\n    fig, (ax_main, ax_hist) = plt.subplots(\n        2, 1, figsize=(4, 4.5), gridspec_kw={'height_ratios': [3, 1]},\n        sharex=True\n    )\n\n    # 主图：gaps\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.7, label='Accuracy', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_confs - bin_accs, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                bottom=bin_accs, color='red', alpha=0.3, label='Gap', zorder=2)\n    ax_main.plot([0, 1], [0, 1], 'k--', linewidth=1, zorder=3, label='Perfect')\n    ax_main.set_xlim(0, 1); ax_main.set_ylim(0, 1)\n    ax_main.set_ylabel('Accuracy')\n    ax_main.set_title(title)\n    ax_main.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9)\n    ax_main.grid(True, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\n\n    # ECE annotation\n    ece = np.nansum(np.array(bin_counts) / sum(bin_counts) *\n                    np.abs(bin_accs - bin_confs))\n    ax_main.text(0.98, 0.02, f'ECE={ece:.3f}', transform=ax_main.transAxes,\n                 ha='right', va='bottom', fontsize=9,\n                 bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.3', facecolor='white', alpha=0.8))\n\n    # 直方图（样本密度）\n    ax_hist.bar(bin_edges[:-1], bin_counts, width=1/n_bins, align='edge',\n                color=color, alpha=0.5)\n    ax_hist.set_ylabel('Count', fontsize=9)\n    ax_hist.set_xlabel('Confidence')\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n### 批量生成 6 张对比图（EMNLP 论文用）\n\n选图策略：每 Regime 选最大 ΔECE 的量化方法 vs FP16，LLaMA + Qwen3 各一。\n\n```python\n# 在 A6000 服务器上执行\nFIGURE_PLAN = [\n    # (dataset, model, quant, regime, title)\n    ('hellaswag',   'llama',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — LLaMA'),\n    ('hellaswag',   'qwen3',  'nf4',  'A', 'HellaSwag (Regime A) — Qwen3'),\n    ('arc_c',       'llama',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — LLaMA'),\n    ('arc_c',       'qwen3',  'gptq', 'B', 'ARC-C (Regime B) — Qwen3'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'llama',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — LLaMA'),\n    ('truthfulqa',  'qwen3',  'awq',  'C', 'TruthfulQA (Regime C) — Qwen3'),\n]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. ECE Heatmap（方法 × 数据集 ΔECE）\n\n**用途**：Main Results Table 的可视化版本，颜色 = ΔECE（红=恶化，蓝=改善）。\n\n```python\ndef plot_ece_heatmap(delta_ece_matrix, methods, datasets,\n                     title='ΔECE Heatmap (vs FP16)',\n                     outpath='ece_heatmap.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    delta_ece_matrix: shape (n_methods, n_datasets), 正=ECE 恶化, 负=改善\n    \"\"\"\n    import matplotlib.colors as mcolors\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(len(datasets) * 1.2, len(methods) * 0.8 + 1))\n\n    # 发散色图（中心=0, 红=恶化, 蓝=改善）\n    vmax = np.nanmax(np.abs(delta_ece_matrix))\n    norm = mcolors.TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-vmax, vcenter=0, vmax=vmax)\n    cmap = plt.cm.RdBu_r\n\n    im = ax.imshow(delta_ece_matrix, cmap=cmap, norm=norm, aspect='auto')\n\n    # 数值标注\n    for i in range(len(methods)):\n        for j in range(len(datasets)):\n            val = delta_ece_matrix[i, j]\n            if not np.isnan(val):\n                color = 'white' if abs(val) > vmax * 0.6 else 'black'\n                sign = '+' if val > 0 else ''\n                ax.text(j, i, f'{sign}{val:.1%}', ha='center', va='center',\n                        fontsize=9, color=color, fontweight='bold')\n\n    ax.set_xticks(range(len(datasets)))\n    ax.set_yticks(range(len(methods)))\n    ax.set_xticklabels(datasets, rotation=30, ha='right')\n    ax.set_yticklabels(methods)\n    ax.set_title(title)\n\n    cbar = plt.colorbar(im, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)\n    cbar.set_label('ΔECE (quantized − FP16)')\n    cbar.ax.axhline(0, color='k', linewidth=0.8)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. T* Scatter Plot（任务 Regime 可视化）\n\n**用途**：展示 5 个模型的 FP16 T* 分布，证明 Regime 跨模型一致性。\n\n```python\ndef plot_tstar_scatter(tstar_data, outpath='tstar_scatter.pdf'):\n    \"\"\"\n    tstar_data: dict of {model_name: {dataset: T*_value}}\n    \"\"\"\n    DATASETS = ['HellaSwag', 'ARC-C', 'MMLU', 'BoolQ', 'TruthfulQA', 'NQ-Open']\n    MODELS = ['LLaMA-3.1-8B', 'Qwen3-4B', 'Gemma-3-12B', 'Mistral-7B', 'Phi-3-mini']\n    REGIME_BANDS = [\n        (0, 1,    '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A\\n(T*<1)'),\n        (1, 5,    '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B\\n(1≤T*<5)'),\n        (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C\\n(T*≥5)'),\n    ]\n\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 4))\n\n    # 背景色区\n    for lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n        ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n        ax.text(5.8, (lo + hi) / 2, label, va='center', fontsize=9,\n                color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n    model_colors = [COLORS.get(m.split('-')[0].lower(), '#333333') for m in MODELS]\n\n    x = np.arange(len(DATASETS))\n    for mi, (model, color) in enumerate(zip(MODELS, model_colors)):\n        y = [tstar_data.get(model, {}).get(ds, np.nan) for ds in DATASETS]\n        ax.plot(x, y, 'o-', color=color, label=model,\n                markersize=7, alpha=0.85, zorder=2)\n\n    ax.set_xticks(x)\n    ax.set_xticklabels(DATASETS, rotation=15, ha='right')\n    ax.set_ylabel('T* (FP16)')\n    ax.set_ylim(0, 10.5)\n    ax.set_title('Calibration Regime by Task — 5 Models (FP16)')\n    ax.axhline(1, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.axhline(5, color='gray', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.legend(loc='upper left', fontsize=9, ncol=2)\n    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.2, axis='y', zorder=1)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig(outpath)\n    plt.close()\n    print(f'Saved: {outpath}')\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. 生成流程（SSH 执行）\n\n```python\n# Step 1: 写脚本到服务器\nssh_execute(\"\"\"cat > /tmp/gen_figures.py << 'PYEOF'\n# ... 粘贴上方代码 + 调用逻辑 ...\nPYEOF\"\"\")\n\n# Step 2: 安装依赖（如有）\nssh_execute(\"pip install matplotlib numpy -q\")\n\n# Step 3: 执行\nssh_execute(\"cd ~/quant-calibration-gap && python /tmp/gen_figures.py\")\n\n# Step 4: PNG → Pattern 实现（按 design_notes 选用）\n\n完整 pattern 目录和何时用见 `paper-visualization.skill.md` L3。这里给可直接复制的实现。\n\n### Pattern A：Regime Background Bands\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\n# 横向 regime bands\nREGIME_BANDS = [(0, 1, '#EBF5FB', 'Regime A'),\n                (1, 5, '#EAFAF1', 'Regime B'),\n                (5, 10.5, '#FDF2F8', 'Regime C')]\nfor lo, hi, color, label in REGIME_BANDS:\n    ax.axhspan(lo, hi, alpha=0.25, color=color, zorder=0)\n    ax.text(x_label_pos, (lo+hi)/2, label, va='center',\n            fontsize=9, color='gray', zorder=1)\n\n# 单一 collapse zone\nax.axhspan(-5, 5, color='#FDEDEC', alpha=0.5, zorder=0)\nax.text(0.02, 2.5, 'collapse zone', fontsize=8,\n        color='#ccaaaa', fontstyle='italic', va='center')\n```\n\n### Pattern B：发散面积 fill_between\n\n```python\n# alpha 极低（0.06）：不抢眼但有面积\nax.fill_between(x_range, curve_upper, curve_lower,\n                alpha=0.06, color='#555555', zorder=1, label='_nolegend_')\n```\n\n### Pattern C：参考线锚定真实值\n\n```python\nax.axhline(y=actual_value, color=color, linestyle=':',\n           linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.3, zorder=1)\nt = ax.text(x_pos, actual_value + 1, 'actual W3A16',\n            fontsize=7, color=color, alpha=0.5, ha='center')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern D：信息层级 marker（hollow vs solid by sample size）\n\n```python\n# 实心 = n >= 50, 空心带 n 标注 = n < 50\nsolid_mask = [n >= 50 for n in n_vals]\nfor x, y, n, solid in zip(xs, ys, n_vals, solid_mask):\n    if solid:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor=color, markeredgewidth=0)\n    else:\n        ax.plot(x, y, marker=marker, color=color, markersize=6,\n                markerfacecolor='white', markeredgecolor=color,\n                markeredgewidth=1.2, zorder=4)\n        ax.annotate(f'n={n}', (x, y), textcoords='offset points',\n                    xytext=(0, 7), fontsize=5, color=color,\n                    ha='center', fontstyle='italic')\n```\n\n### Pattern E：Wilson Binomial CI\n\n```python\ndef wilson_ci(p, n, z=1.96):\n    if n == 0: return 0, 0\n    denom = 1 + z**2 / n\n    center = (p + z**2 / (2*n)) / denom\n    half = z * np.sqrt((p*(1-p) + z**2/(4*n)) / n) / denom\n    return max(0, center - half), min(1, center + half)\n\n# 用法\nlo, hi = wilson_ci(accuracy, n)\nax.errorbar(x, accuracy*100, yerr=[[accuracy*100 - lo*100], [hi*100 - accuracy*100]],\n            fmt='o', capsize=4, capthick=0.8, elinewidth=0.8,\n            markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n```\n\n### Pattern F：Per-label offset dict（防标注重叠）\n\n```python\nlabel_offsets = {\n    'Qwen3-8B':  (6, -12),\n    'Qwen3-4B':  (-55, 4),\n    'Llama-8B':  (6, 6),\n}\nfor name, (x, y) in data.items():\n    ox, oy = label_offsets.get(name, (6, 6))\n    ax.annotate(name, (x, y), xytext=(ox, oy),\n                textcoords='offset points', fontsize=7,\n                arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='-', color='gray',\n                                lw=0.4) if abs(ox) > 20 else None)\n```\n\n### Pattern G：Hatching 加分类维度\n\n```python\nfrom matplotlib.patches import Patch\nfor is_collapse, bar in zip(collapse_flags, bars):\n    if is_collapse:\n        bar.set_hatch('///')\n        bar.set_alpha(0.7)\n# Legend 加 hatching 说明\nlegend_patch = Patch(facecolor='gray', hatch='///', alpha=0.7, label='Collapse')\nax.legend(handles=[legend_patch], loc='lower right')\n```\n\n### Pattern H：Multi-panel 非对称布局\n\n```python\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(6.5, 3.2),\n                          gridspec_kw={'width_ratios': [1.4, 1]})\n# axes[0] 是主面板（1.4x 宽），axes[1] 副面板\n# 给每个 panel 写 insight subtitle\naxes[0].set_title('(a) Quantization shifts confidence', fontsize=9, pad=6)\naxes[1].set_title('(b) Temperature scaling recovers it', fontsize=9, pad=6)\n```\n\n### Pattern I：Broken axis（异常值或对数 0）\n\n```python\nd = 0.012  # 斜线长度\nkwargs = dict(transform=ax.transAxes, color='black',\n              clip_on=False, linewidth=0.6)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (-d, +d), **kwargs)\nax.plot((1-d, 1+d), (1-d, 1+d), **kwargs)\n```\n\n### Pattern J：Path-effect 文字描边\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.patheffects as pe\nt = ax.text(x, y, 'label', fontsize=8, color='red')\nt.set_path_effects([pe.withStroke(linewidth=2.5, foreground='white')])\n```\n\n### Pattern K：Concept curve（合成轨迹建立直觉）\n\n```python\n# 完全不基于实验数据的概念曲线\nx = np.linspace(0, 1, 100)\ntrajectory = baseline * (1 - np.cos(np.pi * x)) / 2\nax.plot(x, trajectory, color='#0072B2', linewidth=2,\n        solid_capstyle='round', label='Theoretical trajectory')\n\n# 关键节点用 insight 标注\nax.annotate('gap eliminated', xy=(1.0, final_y),\n            xytext=(0.7, final_y - 5), color='#388E3C',\n            fontstyle='italic', fontsize=9,\n            arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle='->', color='#388E3C', lw=0.8))\n```\n\n### Pattern L：Forest Plot（meta-analysis 风格）\n\n```python\n# 3-column GridSpec: label | CI plot | inline stats\nfig = plt.figure(figsize=(7, 3.5))\ngs = fig.add_gridspec(1, 3, width_ratios=[0.8, 2, 1.2], wspace=0.1)\nax_label = fig.add_subplot(gs[0]); ax_label.axis('off')\nax_main = fig.add_subplot(gs[1])\nax_stat = fig.add_subplot(gs[2]); ax_stat.axis('off')\n\n# 等价带（半透明 axvspan）\nax_main.axvspan(-1, 1, alpha=0.1, color='gray', label='±1pp equivalence')\n\n# 每行：dot + horizontal CI line\nfor i, (name, mean, lo, hi, pval) in enumerate(rows):\n    ax_label.text(0.5, i, name, fontsize=9, va='center', ha='center')\n    ax_main.plot([lo, hi], [i, i], color='black', linewidth=1)\n    ax_main.plot(mean, i, 'D', color='#0072B2', markersize=7)\n    ax_stat.text(0.5, i, f'{mean:+.2f} [{lo:+.2f}, {hi:+.2f}]  p={pval:.3f}',\n                 fontsize=8, va='center', ha='center', family='monospace')\n\nax_main.axvline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.6)\nax_main.invert_yaxis()\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. 图表 Checklist（提交前）\n\n### L1 防丑（底线）\n- [ ] 300 DPI（`savefig.dpi=300`）+ PDF 矢量\n- [ ] 色盲友好配色（Okabe-Ito 或语义色板）\n- [ ] 所有轴有 label + 单位\n- [ ] 字号 ≥ 10pt（缩到 column width 后仍可读）\n- [ ] 无 dual y-axis、无 3D、无 pie\n- [ ] Bar chart 从 0 起（line/scatter 可非 0）\n- [ ] 文字水平不旋转\n\n### L2 叙事（说服力）\n- [ ] 能用一句话写出 figure takeaway（应等于 `narrative_intent`）\n- [ ] `design_notes` 中的 pattern 都已实现\n- [ ] Subtitle / panel label 是 insight 不是描述\n- [ ] 数据质量差异视觉可见（n < 50 用空心 / OOS 用 diamond 等）\n- [ ] 颜色绑定语义而非数据顺序（整篇论文一致）\n\n### L3 美观（高级感）\n- [ ] 不像 matplotlib gallery 示例（无默认灰背景、无粗网格、无 tab10 色板）\n- [ ] 配色 muted 和谐，不刺眼（用 Okabe-Ito 或自定义语义色板）\n- [ ] 线宽、字号、间距全图统一\n- [ ] 元素对齐严格（bars 底部对齐、labels 水平对齐、panels 顶部对齐）\n- [ ] 留白充足（元素之间不拥挤，panels 之间有间距）\n- [ ] 与论文其他图的风格一致（配色方案、字号、线宽）\n- [ ] Hero Figure（Figure 1 + 主结果图）投入了 ≥5 轮迭代\n- [ ] 终极判断：这张图放在 NeurIPS best paper 里违和吗？\n","numLines":518,"startLine":1,"totalLines":518}}}
